<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs仅基于最终结果训练，将视觉推理视为黑盒，依赖表面模式匹配而非严谨的多步推理。将可验证奖励的强化学习扩展到视觉领域，因连续像素空间中抽象逻辑的模糊性而受阻。

Method: 利用表格的确定性网格结构作为理想视觉测试平台；采用专门评论家VLM对策略VLM生成的显式视觉思维链提供密集的步骤级反馈；提出PGPO算法，整合过程奖励、解耦策略约束和长度感知动态采样。

Result: V-tableR1 4B在复杂表格基准上达到开源SOTA，超越18倍大小的模型，性能优于其SFT基线。明确惩罚视觉幻觉和捷径猜测。

Conclusion: 通过将多模态推理从黑盒模式匹配转变为可验证的逻辑推导，V-tableR1实现了更严谨、可解释的视觉推理，证明了过程监督在视觉领域RL中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce V-tableR1, a process-supervised reinforcement learning framework that elicits rigorous, verifiable reasoning from multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Current MLLMs trained solely on final outcomes often treat visual reasoning as a black box, relying on superficial pattern matching rather than performing rigorous multi-step inference. While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards could enforce transparent reasoning trajectories, extending it to visual domains remains severely hindered by the ambiguity of grounding abstract logic into continuous pixel space. We solve this by leveraging the deterministic grid structure of tables as an ideal visual testbed. V-tableR1 employs a specialized critic VLM to provide dense, step-level feedback on the explicit visual chain-of-thought generated by a policy VLM. To optimize this system, we propose Process-Guided Direct Alignment Policy Optimization (PGPO), a novel RL algorithm integrating process rewards, decoupled policy constraints, and length-aware dynamic sampling. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that V-tableR1 explicitly penalizes visual hallucinations and shortcut guessing. By fundamentally shifting multimodal inference from black-box pattern matching to verifiable logical derivation, V-tableR1 4B establishes state-of-the-art accuracy among open-source models on complex tabular benchmarks, outperforming models up to 18x its size and improving over its SFT baseline

</details>


### [148] [SWE-chat: Coding Agent Interactions From Real Users in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20779)
*Joachim Baumann,Vishakh Padmakumar,Xiang Li,John Yang,Diyi Yang,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SWE-chat，首个大规模真实编码智能体会话数据集，包含6000个会话、63000+用户提示和355000+工具调用。研究发现：编码模式呈双峰分布（41%由AI主导，23%完全由人类编写），仅44%的AI生成代码被保留在提交中，且AI代码引入更多安全漏洞。用户在44%的轮次中对抗AI输出（纠正、报错、中断）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体已被大规模采用，但缺乏对真实使用模式和实际效用的实证证据，现有研究多基于人工基准而非真实开发场景。

Method: 构建SWE-chat数据集，自动持续从公开仓库收集编码智能体会话，包含完整的用户-智能体交互轨迹及代码归属标注，并进行实证分析。

Result: 编码模式呈双峰分布（41%“共鸣编码”vs 23%纯手工编码）；仅44%的AI生成代码被保留到最终提交；AI代码引入更多安全漏洞；用户在44%的轮次中对AI输出做出对抗性反应。

Conclusion: 尽管AI编码智能体能力快速提升，但在真实场景中效率有限，安全性和用户采纳率仍有显著问题。SWE-chat为超越人工基准、基于证据理解AI智能体在真实开发流程中的表现提供了实证基础。

Abstract: AI coding agents are being adopted at scale, yet we lack empirical evidence on how people actually use them and how much of their output is useful in practice. We present SWE-chat, the first large-scale dataset of real coding agent sessions collected from open-source developers in the wild. The dataset currently contains 6,000 sessions, comprising more than 63,000 user prompts and 355,000 agent tool calls. SWE-chat is a living dataset; our collection pipeline automatically and continually discovers and processes sessions from public repositories. Leveraging SWE-chat, we provide an initial empirical characterization of real-world coding agent usage and failure modes. We find that coding patterns are bimodal: in 41% of sessions, agents author virtually all committed code ("vibe coding"), while in 23%, humans write all code themselves. Despite rapidly improving capabilities, coding agents remain inefficient in natural settings. Just 44% of all agent-produced code survives into user commits, and agent-written code introduces more security vulnerabilities than code authored by humans. Furthermore, users push back against agent outputs -- through corrections, failure reports, and interruptions -- in 44% of all turns. By capturing complete interaction traces with human vs. agent code authorship attribution, SWE-chat provides an empirical foundation for moving beyond curated benchmarks towards an evidence-based understanding of how AI agents perform in real developer workflows.

</details>


### [149] [Automatic Ontology Construction Using LLMs as an External Layer of Memory, Verification, and Planning for Hybrid Intelligent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20795)
*Pavel Salovskii,Iuliia Gorshkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合智能系统架构，将大语言模型与外部本体记忆层（RDF/OWL知识图谱）结合，通过自动化本体构建管道实现持久化、可验证的语义推理，在规划任务上优于纯LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM系统存在缺乏长期记忆、结构理解弱、推理能力有限等关键缺陷，单纯依赖参数化知识和向量检索（RAG）不足以解决这些问题。

Method: 构建自动化本体管道：从异构数据源进行实体识别、关系抽取、归一化和三元组生成，经SHACL/OWL约束验证后持续更新图结构。推理时LLM融合向量检索、图推理和外部工具交互。

Result: 在汉诺塔等多步推理规划任务上，本体增强系统相比基线LLM系统表现更优，并且本体层支持对输出的形式化验证，形成生成-验证-校正流水线。

Conclusion: 该架构解决了LLM的长期记忆、结构理解和推理能力不足问题，为需要持久知识、可解释性和可靠决策的智能体系统、机器人应用和企业AI提供了基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a hybrid architecture for intelligent systems in which large language models (LLMs) are extended with an external ontological memory layer. Instead of relying solely on parametric knowledge and vector-based retrieval (RAG), the proposed approach constructs and maintains a structured knowledge graph using RDF/OWL representations, enabling persistent, verifiable, and semantically grounded reasoning.
  The core contribution is an automated pipeline for ontology construction from heterogeneous data sources, including documents, APIs, and dialogue logs. The system performs entity recognition, relation extraction, normalization, and triple generation, followed by validation using SHACL and OWL constraints, and continuous graph updates. During inference, LLMs operate over a combined context that integrates vector-based retrieval with graph-based reasoning and external tool interaction.
  Experimental observations on planning tasks, including the Tower of Hanoi benchmark, indicate that ontology augmentation improves performance in multi-step reasoning scenarios compared to baseline LLM systems. In addition, the ontology layer enables formal validation of generated outputs, transforming the system into a generation-verification-correction pipeline.
  The proposed architecture addresses key limitations of current LLM-based systems, including lack of long-term memory, weak structural understanding, and limited reasoning capabilities. It provides a foundation for building agent-based systems, robotics applications, and enterprise AI solutions that require persistent knowledge, explainability, and reliable decision-making.

</details>


### [150] [Diagnosing CFG Interpretation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20811)
*Hanqi Li,Lu Chen,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估LLM作为上下文解释器的能力，通过RoboGrid框架解耦语法、行为与语义，发现LLM存在层级退化：能维持表面语法但丢失结构语义，在深度递归和高分支下性能崩塌，且依赖关键词语义引导而非纯符号归纳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被集成到智能体系统中，必须遵循动态定义的、机器可解释的接口。现有研究缺乏对LLM在理解上下文无关文法方面的系统评估，尤其是在语法、行为和语义三个维度上的解耦分析。

Method: 提出了RoboGrid框架，通过控制递归深度、表达式复杂度和表面风格等变量，对LLM进行压力测试，解耦语法有效性、行为功能性和语义忠实性三个维度。

Result: 1) LLM存在一致的层级退化：表面语法保持良好但结构语义丢失严重；2) CoT推理只能部分缓解问题；3) 在深度递归和高分支的结构密度下性能崩溃；4) "异形"词汇实验表明LLM依赖关键词的语义引导而非纯符号归纳。

Conclusion: LLM在层级状态追踪方面存在关键缺陷，这限制了它们作为可靠、语法无关的智能体的能力。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly integrated into agentic systems, they must adhere to dynamically defined, machine-interpretable interfaces. We evaluate LLMs as in-context interpreters: given a novel context-free grammar, can LLMs generate syntactically valid, behaviorally functional, and semantically faithful outputs? We introduce RoboGrid, a framework that disentangles syntax, behavior, and semantics through controlled stress-tests of recursion depth, expression complexity, and surface styles. Our experiments reveal a consistent hierarchical degradation: LLMs often maintain surface syntax but fail to preserve structural semantics. Despite the partial mitigation provided by CoT reasoning, performance collapses under structural density, specifically deep recursion and high branching, with semantic alignment vanishing at extreme depths. Furthermore, "Alien" lexicons reveal that LLMs rely on semantic bootstrapping from keywords rather than pure symbolic induction. These findings pinpoint critical gaps in hierarchical state-tracking required for reliable, grammar-agnostic agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [SL(C)AMma: Simultaneous Localisation, (Calibration) and Mapping With a Magnetometer Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19946)
*Thomas Edridge,Manon Kok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于磁传感器阵列的SLAM算法（SLAMma和SLCAMma），通过滤波方法解决室内定位中磁力计不一致性问题，实现超80%的漂移减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内定位中GNSS信号衰减，且仅依赖本体感知传感器会导致漂移累积；现有磁场SLAM在探索未知区域时仍存在挑战，且磁力计阵列中传感器间测量不一致会影响里程计估计和闭环检测。

Method: 提出两种滤波算法：SLAMma（基于磁传感器阵列的磁场SLAM）和SLCAMma（联合估计磁力计校准参数）。通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证校准参数在充分方向激励下的可估计性，并在10个数据集上实验验证。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明：充分方向激励下可准确估计校准参数，且磁力计阵列能实现传感器间测量一致性。实验显示：在单磁力计SLAM失败场景下，SLAMma和SLCAMma能提供良好的轨迹估计，相比本体感知传感器积分减少80%以上漂移。

Conclusion: 磁传感器阵列结合所提滤波算法可有效解决室内定位中的漂移累积问题，显著优于单磁力计SLAM方案。

Abstract: Indoor localisation techniques suffer from attenuated Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals and from the accumulation of unbounded drift by integration of proprioceptive sensors. Magnetic field-based Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping (SLAM) reduces drift through loop closures by revisiting previously seen locations, but extended exploration of unseen areas remains challenging. Recently, magnetometer arrays have demonstrated significant benefits over single magnetometers, as they can directly estimate the odometry. However, inconsistencies between magnetometer measurements negatively affect odometry estimates and complicate loop closure detection. We propose two filtering algorithms: The first focuses on magnetic field-based SLAM using a magnetometer array (SLAMma). The second extends this to jointly estimate the magnetometer calibration parameters (SLCAMma). We demonstrate, using Monte Carlo simulations, that the calibration parameters can be accurately estimated when there is sufficient orientation excitation, and that magnetometers achieve inter-sensor measurement consistency regardless of the type of motion. Experimental validation on ten datasets confirms these results, and we demonstrate that in cases where single magnetometer SLAM fails, SLAMma and SLCAMma provide good trajectory estimates with, more than 80% drift reduction compared to integration of proprioceptive sensors.

</details>


### [152] [Radar Odometry Subject to High Tilt Dynamics of Subarctic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19962)
*Matěj Boxan,William Larivée-Hardy,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对亚北极动态地形中旋转FMCW雷达里程计面临的挑战，比较了三种现有方法并提出了一种新的雷达-惯性里程计方法。新方法采用倾斜邻近子图搜索和垂直位移硬阈值技术，在城市基准上达到最先进性能，并在2公里动态轨迹上比第二名方法提升0.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有旋转FMCW雷达里程计方法通常假设平坦地面，这在城市环境或平坦矿区中可行，但在亚北极等高度动态地形中（连续扫描间俯仰差达13°，横滚差达4°），标准特征提取和状态估计技术面临严峻挑战，因此需要针对非平坦动态地形的鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的雷达-惯性里程计方法，包含两个核心技术：(1) 倾斜邻近子图搜索（tilt-proximity submap search），用于应对地形的大角度变化；(2) 硬阈值机制（hard threshold），用于处理扫描点与估计旋转轴之间的垂直位移，从而提升动态地形下的状态估计鲁棒性。

Result: 在城市基准数据集上达到了最先进（state-of-the-art）性能；在2公里长的动态轨迹上，比第二名方法提升了0.3%的精度。此外，在高横向滑移和陡峭沟渠穿越的复杂雷达序列上，对四种方法进行了性能对比分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的雷达-惯性里程计方法在亚北极动态地形中表现出优越的性能，验证了倾斜邻近子图搜索和垂直位移硬阈值技术在非平坦地面场景中的有效性，为雷达里程计在复杂地形中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Rotating FMCW radar odometry methods often assume flat ground conditions. While this assumption is sufficient in many scenarios, including urban environments or flat mining setups, the highly dynamic terrain of subarctic environments poses a challenge to standard feature extraction and state estimation techniques. This paper benchmarks three existing radar odometry methods under demanding conditions, exhibiting up to 13° in pitch and 4° in roll difference between consecutive scans, with absolute pitch and roll reaching 30° and 8°, respectively. Furthermore, we propose a novel radar-inertial odometry method utilizing tilt-proximity submap search and a hard threshold for vertical displacement between scan points and the estimated axis of rotation. Experimental results demonstrate a state-of-the-art performance of our method on an urban baseline and a 0.3% improvement over the second-best comparative method on a 2-kilometer-long dynamic trajectory. Finally, we analyze the performance of the four evaluated methods on a complex radar sequence characterized by high lateral slip and a steep ditch traversal.

</details>


### [153] [Efficient Reinforcement Learning using Linear Koopman Dynamics for Nonlinear Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19980)
*Wenjian Hao,Yuxuan Fang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Koopman算子理论的学习型 lifted 动力学模型与actor-critic架构相结合的模型驱动强化学习框架，用于非线性机器人系统的最优闭环控制。通过一步预测估计策略梯度，在多个仿真和两个真实机器人平台上验证了其样本效率和控制性能优于多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型驱动强化学习方法在多步模型展开中计算成本高且累积误差大，而模型无关方法样本效率低。本文旨在结合Koopman算子理论的线性化优势与actor-critic架构的策略优化能力，实现高效、低成本的非线性机器人控制。

Method: 利用Koopman算子理论将非线性动力学提升到线性 lifted 空间学习模型；将所学线性模型集成到actor-critic架构中；采用一步预测（而非多步传播）估计策略梯度，形成在线小批量策略梯度框架，从流式交互数据中持续改进策略。

Result: 在多个仿真非线性控制基准任务以及真实Kinova Gen3机械臂和Unitree Go1四足机器人上实验，结果表明：相比模型无关强化学习基线，样本效率显著提升；相比其他模型驱动强化学习基线，控制性能更优；性能接近依赖精确系统动力学的经典模型驱动方法。

Conclusion: 基于Koopman算子的一步预测策略梯度框架能有效降低计算代价和模型展开误差，在真实机器人平台上实现高效、高性能的闭环控制，为模型驱动强化学习在非线性系统控制中的应用提供了实用且有效的方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a model-based reinforcement learning (RL) framework for optimal closed-loop control of nonlinear robotic systems. The proposed approach learns linear lifted dynamics through Koopman operator theory and integrates the resulting model into an actor-critic architecture for policy optimization, where the policy represents a parameterized closed-loop controller. To reduce computational cost and mitigate model rollout errors, policy gradients are estimated using one-step predictions of the learned dynamics rather than multi-step propagation. This leads to an online mini-batch policy gradient framework that enables policy improvement from streamed interaction data. The proposed framework is evaluated on several simulated nonlinear control benchmarks and two real-world hardware platforms, including a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm and a Unitree Go1 quadruped. Experimental results demonstrate improved sample efficiency over model-free RL baselines, superior control performance relative to model-based RL baselines, and control performance comparable to classical model-based methods that rely on exact system dynamics.

</details>


### [154] [Strain in Sound: Soft Corrugated Tube for Local Strain Sensing with Acoustic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20017)
*Michael Chun,Ananya Nukala,Tae Myung Huh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于软波纹管传感器的分段应变估计方法，通过气流激励产生声学共振，利用机器学习实现高精度应变测量，在假肢手指上验证了多关节配置的区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人的变形感知是一个挑战，现有方法难以实现非均匀变形的局部应变估计，因此需要一种新型传感方案。

Method: 设计软波纹管传感器，通过气流激发管内驻波共振，采集不同拉伸条件下的共振频率数据，使用梯度提升回归器进行机器学习训练，实现分段应变估计。

Result: 双周期管（3.1mm和4.18mm）MAE为0.8mm，单周期管（3.1mm）MAE为1mm，在假肢手指上成功区分多关节配置。

Conclusion: 该传感器能有效估计软体中的非均匀变形，具有在软体机器人中应用的潜力。

Abstract: We present a soft corrugated tube sensor designed to estimate strain in each half segment. When air flows through the tube, the internal corrugated cavities induce pressure oscillations that excite the tube's standing wave resonance mode, generating an acoustic tone. Stretching the tube affects both the resonance mode frequency, due to changes in overall length, and the frequency-flow speed relationship, due to variations in cavity width, which is particularly useful for local strain estimation. By sweeping flow rates in a controlled manner, we collected resonance frequency data across flow speeds under various local stretch conditions, enabling a machine learning algorithm (gradient boosting regressor) to estimate segmental strain with high accuracy. The dual-period tube design (3.1 mm and 4.18 mm corrugation periods) achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.8 mm, while the single-period tube (3.1 mm) provided a satisfactory MAE of 1 mm. Testing on a mannequin finger demonstrated the sensor's capability to differentiate multi-joint configurations, showing its potential for estimating non-uniform deformations in soft bodies.

</details>


### [155] [JoyAI-RA 0.1: A Foundation Model for Robotic Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20100)
*Tianle Zhang,Zhihao Yuan,Dafeng Chi,Peidong Liu,Dongwei Li,Kejun Hu,Likui Zhang,Junnan Nie,Ziming Wei,Zengjue Chen,Yili Tang,Jiayi Li,Zhiyuan Xiang,Mingyang Li,Tianci Luo,Hanwen Wan,Ao Li,Linbo Zhai,Zhihao Zhan,Yuzheng Zhuang,Liang Lin,Xiaodong Bai,Jiakun Cai,Peng Cao,Kangliang Chen,Siang Chen,Yixiang Dai,Shuai Di,Nan Duan,Yicheng Gong,Chenguang Gui,Yucheng Guo,Peng Hao,Qingrong He,Haoyang Huang,Kunrui Huang,Zhixuan Huang,Shibo Jin,Yixiang Jin,Anson Li,Dongjiang Li,Jiawei Li,Ruodai Li,Yihang Li,Yuzhen Li,Jiaming Liang,Fangsheng Liu,Jing Long,Mingxi Luo,Xing Pan,Hui Shen,Xiaomeng Tian,Daming Wang,Song Wang,Junwu Xiong,Hang Xu,Wanting Xu,Zhengcheng Yu,He Zhang,Jiyao Zhang,Lin Zhao,Chen Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: JoyAI-RA是一个视觉-语言-动作（VLA）具身基础模型，通过多源多层级预训练框架，统一异构数据（网络数据、人类操作视频、仿真轨迹、真实机器人数据）的动作空间，实现跨本体的行为学习，在仿真和真实环境中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人数据集规模有限、任务覆盖不足，且不同机器人本体之间存在较大差异，阻碍了行为知识的有效迁移，导致开放世界中的机器人自主性受到限制。

Method: 提出JoyAI-RA，一个VLA具身基础模型，采用多源多层级预训练框架，整合网络数据、大规模第一人称人类操作视频、仿真生成轨迹和真实机器人数据，通过显式动作空间统一来弥合本体差距（尤其是人类操作与机器人控制之间）。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界基准测试中，JoyAI-RA均优于现有最先进方法，尤其是在需要泛化能力的多样化任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: 多源多层级预训练结合动作空间统一能够有效解决数据多样性不足和跨本体泛化问题，为开放世界机器人操作提供了可行的基础模型方案。

Abstract: Robotic autonomy in open-world environments is fundamentally limited by insufficient data diversity and poor cross-embodiment generalization. Existing robotic datasets are often limited in scale and task coverage, while relatively large differences across robot embodiments impede effective behavior knowledge transfer. To address these challenges, we propose JoyAI-RA, a vision-language-action (VLA) embodied foundation model tailored for generalizable robotic manipulation. JoyAI-RA presents a multi-source multi-level pretraining framework that integrates web data, large-scale egocentric human manipulation videos, simulation-generated trajectories, and real-robot data. Through training on heterogeneous multi-source data with explicit action-space unification, JoyAI-RA effectively bridges embodiment gaps, particularly between human manipulation and robotic control, thereby enhancing cross-embodiment behavior learning. JoyAI-RA outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both simulation and real-world benchmarks, especially on diverse tasks with generalization demands.

</details>


### [156] [Toward Safe Autonomous Robotic Endovascular Interventions using World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20151)
*Harry Robertshaw,Nikola Fischer,Han-Ru Wu,Andrea Walker Perez,Weiyuan Deng,Benjamin Jackson,Christos Bergeles,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于世界模型（TD-MPC2）的自主机械取栓导航框架，在仿真中成功率显著优于SAC（58% vs 36%），体外实验中成功率相当但路径比更优，首次在多种患者特异性血管中进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机械取栓面临血管高度变异性和实时精准控制的挑战，现有强化学习方法在面对多样化患者解剖结构或长导航路径时鲁棒性不足。

Method: 采用基于模型的强化学习方法TD-MPC2，集成规划与学习动力学，在多种患者特异性血管模型上训练，并与SAC算法进行对比，在仿真和体外荧光镜引导下验证。

Result: 仿真中TD-MPC2成功率58%显著高于SAC的36%（p<0.001），接触力均值0.15N远低于破裂阈值1.5N；体外实验中TD-MPC2成功率68%与SAC的60%无显著差异，但路径比更优（p=0.017），手术时间更长（p<0.001）。

Conclusion: 这是首次在多种保留的患者特异性血管数据和荧光镜引导体外实验中验证自主MT导航，表明世界模型有望实现安全且泛化的AI辅助血管内介入。

Abstract: Autonomous mechanical thrombectomy (MT) presents substantial challenges due to highly variable vascular geometries and the requirements for accurate, real-time control. While reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for the automation of endovascular navigation, existing approaches often show limited robustness when faced with diverse patient anatomies or extended navigation horizons. In this work, we investigate a world-model-based framework for autonomous endovascular navigation built on TD-MPC2, a model-based RL method that integrates planning and learned dynamics. We evaluate a TD-MPC2 agent trained on multiple navigation tasks across hold out patient-specific vasculatures and benchmark its performance against the state-of-the-art Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm agent. Both approaches are further validated in vitro using patient-specific vascular phantoms under fluoroscopic guidance. In simulation, TD-MPC2 demonstrates a significantly higher mean success rate than SAC (58% vs. 36%, p < 0.001), and mean tip contact forces of 0.15 N, well below the proposed 1.5 N vessel rupture threshold. Mean success rates for TD-MPC2 (68%) were comparable to SAC (60%) in vitro, but TD-MPC2 achieved superior path ratios (p = 0.017) at the cost of longer procedure times (p < 0.001). Together, these results provide the first demonstration of autonomous MT navigation validated across both hold out in silico data and fluoroscopy-guided in vitro experiments, highlighting the promise of world models for safe and generalizable AI-assisted endovascular interventions.

</details>


### [157] [LLM-Guided Safety Agent for Edge Robotics with an ISO-Compliant Perception-Compute-Control Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20193)
*Xu Huang,Ruofan Zhang,Lu Cheng,Yuefeng Song,Xu Huang,Huayu Zhang,Sheng Yin,Anyang Liang,Chen Qian,Yin Zhou,Xiaoyun Yuan,Yuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的安全智能体架构，通过将自然语言安全法规转化为可执行谓词，并在符合ISO标准的高冗余边缘计算平台上实现人机交互中的功能安全，在低成本硬件上实现了ISO 13849 Category 3和PL d等级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI感知本质上是概率性的，而工业标准要求确定性行为，这种矛盾使得人机交互中的功能安全极具挑战性。

Method: 提出LLM引导的安全智能体，将自然语言安全法规翻译为可执行谓词；采用对称双模冗余设计，在异构边缘运行环境中实现并行独立的低延迟感知、计算和控制；部署在双RK3588平台上。

Result: 在代表性人机交互场景中验证了系统有效性，证明在低成本硬件上可实现ISO 13849 Category 3和PL d安全等级。

Conclusion: 该工作为安全关键的具身AI在实际边缘部署提供了可行路径，展示了LLM在弥合概率性AI与确定性安全标准之间鸿沟的潜力。

Abstract: Ensuring functional safety in human-robot interaction is challenging because AI perception is inherently probabilistic, whereas industrial standards require deterministic behavior. We present an LLM-guided safety agent for edge robotics, built on an ISO-compliant low-latency perception-compute-control architecture. Our method translates natural-language safety regulations into executable predicates and deploys them through a redundant heterogeneous edge runtime. For fault-tolerant closed-loop execution under edge constraints, we adopt a symmetric dual-modular redundancy design with parallel independent execution for low-latency perception, computation, and control. We prototype the system on a dual-RK3588 platform and evaluate it in representative human-robot interaction scenarios. The results demonstrate a practical edge implementation path toward ISO 13849 Category 3 and PL d using cost-effective hardware, supporting practical deployment of safety-critical embodied AI.

</details>


### [158] [Stochastic Barrier Certificates in the Presence of Dynamic Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20208)
*Rayan Mazouz,Luca Laurenti,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过随机障碍函数为受不确定性影响的离散时间、连续空间随机动态系统提供安全保证，提出时不变和时变障碍证书，后者利用Bellman最优性得到更紧的安全概率下界，并通过平方和规划实现高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理动态障碍物环境下的随机系统安全验证时，安全边界过于保守，且未能有效利用时间结构信息。

Method: 提出时变障碍证书，基于Bellman最优性原理捕捉时间依赖结构；将多项式形式的证书构造转化为凸的平方和（SOS）规划问题。

Result: 在含动态障碍物的非线性系统上，时变证书相较现有方法获得更紧的安全保证，精度和可扩展性均有提升。

Conclusion: 所提时变障碍证书方法能够有效降低保守性，为动态障碍环境下的随机安全验证提供更精确、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Safety of stochastic dynamic systems in environments with dynamic obstacles is studied in this paper through the lens of stochastic barrier functions. We introduce both time-invariant and time-varying barrier certificates for discrete-time, continuous-space systems subject to uncertainty, which provide certified lower bounds on the probability of remaining within a safe set over a finite horizon. These certificates explicitly account for time-varying unsafe regions induced by obstacle dynamics. By leveraging Bellman's optimality perspective, the time-varying formulation directly captures temporal structure and yields less conservative bounds than state-of-the-art approaches. By restricting certificates to polynomial functions, we show that time-varying barrier synthesis can be formulated as a convex sum-of-squares program, enabling tractable optimization. Empirical evaluations on nonlinear systems with dynamic obstacles show that time-varying certificates consistently achieve tight guarantees, demonstrating improved accuracy and scalability over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [159] [Toward Cooperative Driving in Mixed Traffic: An Adaptive Potential Game-Based Approach with Field Test Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20231)
*Shiyu Fang,Xiaocong Zhao,Xuekai Liu,Peng Hang,Jianqiang Wang,Yunpeng Wang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应势博弈（APG）协作驾驶框架，通过Shapley值量化个体边际贡献并动态估计人类驾驶行为偏好，实现混合交通流中安全高效的协同决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作驾驶方法忽视参与者个体需求与异质性，难以推广至人机混行环境。

Method: 基于势博弈构建个体与系统联合效用函数；引入Shapley值量化车辆边际贡献；通过观测行为与模型估计的差异动态更新人类驾驶偏好。

Result: 消融实验证明自适应更新显著提升混合交通协作成功率；对比实验显示APG在安全性与效率上优于其他协作方法；实地测试验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: APG框架有效解决混合交通中个体异质性问题，兼顾系统整体最优与个体需求，具备现实部署潜力。

Abstract: Connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs), which represent a significant advancement in autonomous driving technology, have the potential to greatly increase traffic safety and efficiency through cooperative decision-making. However, existing methods often overlook the individual needs and heterogeneity of cooperative participants, making it difficult to transfer them to environments where they coexist with human-driven vehicles (HDVs).To address this challenge, this paper proposes an adaptive potential game (APG) cooperative driving framework. First, the system utility function is established on the basis of a general form of individual utility and its monotonic relationship, allowing for the simultaneous optimization of both individual and system objectives. Second, the Shapley value is introduced to compute each vehicle's marginal utility within the system, allowing its varying impact to be quantified. Finally, the HDV preference estimation is dynamically refined by continuously comparing the observed HDV behavior with the APG's estimated actions, leading to improvements in overall system safety and efficiency. Ablation studies demonstrate that adaptively updating Shapley values and HDV preference estimation significantly improve cooperation success rates in mixed traffic. Comparative experiments further highlight the APG's advantages in terms of safety and efficiency over other cooperative methods. Moreover, the applicability of the approach to real-world scenarios was validated through field tests.

</details>


### [160] [Cortex 2.0: Grounding World Models in Real-World Industrial Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20246)
*Adriana Aida,Walida Amer,Katarina Bankovic,Dhruv Behl,Fabian Busch,Annie Bhalla,Minh Duong,Florian Gienger,Rohan Godse,Denis Grachev,Ralf Gulde,Elisa Hagensieker,Junpeng Hu,Shivam Joshi,Tobias Knoblauch,Likith Kumar,Damien LaRocque,Keerthana Lokesh,Omar Moured,Khiem Nguyen,Christian Preyss,Ranjith Sriganesan,Vikram Singh,Carsten Sponner,Anh Tong,Dominik Tuscher,Marc Tuscher,Pavan Upputuri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cortex 2.0提出从反应式控制转向"规划-执行"范式，通过生成未来轨迹并评分选择最优路径，在四项工业操作任务上显著优于现有VLA模型，尤其在杂乱、遮挡等非结构化环境中表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型本质上是反应式的（仅基于当前观测优化下一步动作），缺乏对未来结果的评估，导致在长程任务中容易出现累积性失败。因此需要一种能够预见未来并主动规划的操控范式。

Method: 提出Cortex 2.0框架，核心思想是"规划-执行"（plan-and-act）：在视觉潜在空间中生成候选的未来轨迹，对每条轨迹进行预期成功率和效率的评分，最后只执行得分最高的轨迹。

Result: 在单臂和双臂操作平台上对四项任务（抓取放置、物品分类、螺丝分拣、鞋盒开箱）进行测试，Cortex 2.0在所有任务中均优于SOTA VLA基线，在重度杂乱、频繁遮挡和接触密集操作的非结构化环境中仍保持可靠。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型的规划方法可以在复杂工业环境中可靠运行，为长程工业操作提供了一种超越反应式控制的可行路径。

Abstract: Industrial robotic manipulation demands reliable long-horizon execution across embodiments, tasks, and changing object distributions. While Vision-Language-Action models have demonstrated strong generalization, they remain fundamentally reactive. By optimizing the next action given the current observation without evaluating potential futures, they are brittle to the compounding failure modes of long-horizon tasks. Cortex 2.0 shifts from reactive control to plan-and-act by generating candidate future trajectories in visual latent space, scoring them for expected success and efficiency, then committing only to the highest-scoring candidate. We evaluate Cortex 2.0 on a single-arm and dual-arm manipulation platform across four tasks of increasing complexity: pick and place, item and trash sorting, screw sorting, and shoebox unpacking. Cortex 2.0 consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Vision-Language-Action baselines, achieving the best results across all tasks. The system remains reliable in unstructured environments characterized by heavy clutter, frequent occlusions, and contact-rich manipulation, where reactive policies fail. These results demonstrate that world-model-based planning can operate reliably in complex industrial environments.

</details>


### [161] [Onboard Wind Estimation for Small UAVs Equipped with Low-Cost Sensors: An Aerodynamic Model-Integrated Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20290)
*Bingchen Cheng,Tielin Ma,Jingcheng Fu,Lulu Tao,Tianhui Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅利用小型无人机低成本机载传感器（无需额外测风设备）即可实现自主风速估计的方法，该方法结合扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）与空气动力学模型及自适应移动平均估计（AMAE）技术，能有效估计稳态与时变三维风矢量，并通过仿真和飞行试验验证了其有效性、实时性及实际应用可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现小型无人机（UAV）的节能飞行，需要自主估计环境风速，但传统方法依赖额外风测量设备，增加了成本与载荷。因此，研究旨在仅利用自主飞行所必需的廉价传感器来实现精确的风场估计。

Method: 提出一种结合扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）与空气动力学模型的方法，并引入自适应移动平均估计（AMAE）技术，以提高风估计的精度和平滑度。该方法无需流动角度测量即可实现三维风矢量估计。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法能有效估计稳态和时变三维风矢量；飞行试验验证了方法的有效性及实时机载计算的可行性；同时分析了空气动力学模型精度对风估计误差的影响，并系统检验了测试中的不确定性与误差源。

Conclusion: 所提方法仅利用低成本机载传感器即可实现可靠的三维风场估计，为小型无人机节能飞行提供了实用方案，未来可基于识别的误差源进一步优化。

Abstract: To enable autonomous wind estimation for energy-efficient flight in small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), this study proposes a method that estimates flight states and wind using only the low-cost essential onboard sensors required for autonomous flight, without relying on additional wind measurement devices. The core of the method includes an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) integrated with the aerodynamic model and an Adaptive Moving Average Estimation (AMAE) technique, which improves the accuracy and smoothness of the wind estimation. Simulation results show that the approach efficiently estimates both steady and time-varying 3D wind vectors without requiring flow angle measurements. The impact of aerodynamic model accuracy on wind estimation errors is also analyzed to assess practical applicability. Flight tests validate the effectiveness of the method and its feasibility for real-time onboard computation. Additionally, uncertainties and error sources encountered during testing are systematically examined, providing a foundation for further refinement.

</details>


### [162] [ETac: A Lightweight and Efficient Tactile Simulation Framework for Learning Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20295)
*Zhe Xu,Feiyu Zhao,Xiyan Huang,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ETac是一个高效高保真的触觉仿真框架，通过轻量数据驱动变形传播模型模拟软体接触动力学，支持大规模并行策略训练，在盲抓取任务中达到84.45%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体触觉仿真在保真度与计算成本之间存在权衡，限制了基于触觉的灵巧操作策略学习的大规模应用。

Method: 提出ETac框架，采用轻量级数据驱动变形传播模型模拟弹性体接触动力学；作为仿真后端，同时支持4096个并行环境的高效强化学习训练。

Result: 在单张RTX 4090 GPU上实现869 FPS的吞吐量；训练的盲抓取策略在四种物体上平均成功率达84.45%。

Conclusion: ETac证明了触觉技能学习可以实现高效、可扩展，为大规模触觉策略训练提供了可行的仿真方案。

Abstract: Tactile sensors are increasingly integrated into dexterous robotic manipulators to enhance contact perception. However, learning manipulation policies that rely on tactile sensing remains challenging, primarily due to the trade-off between fidelity and computational cost of soft-body simulations. To address this, we present ETac, a tactile simulation framework that models elastomeric soft-body interactions with both high fidelity and efficiency. ETac employs a lightweight data-driven deformation propagation model to capture soft-body contact dynamics, achieving high simulation quality and boosting efficiency that enables large-scale policy training. When serving as the simulation backend, ETac produces surface deformation estimates comparable to FEM and demonstrates applicability for modeling real tactile sensors. Then, we showcase its capability in training a blind grasping policy that leverages large-area tactile feedback to manipulate diverse objects. Running on a single RTX 4090 GPU, ETac supports reinforcement learning across 4,096 parallel environments, achieving a total throughput of 869 FPS. The resulting policy reaches an average success rate of 84.45% across four object types, underscoring ETac's potential to make tactile-based skill learning both efficient and scalable.

</details>


### [163] [AdaTracker: Learning Adaptive In-Context Policy for Cross-Embodiment Active Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20305)
*Kui Wu,Hao Chen,Jinzhu Han,Haijun Liu,Churan Wang,Yizhou Wang,Zhoujun Li,Si Liu,Fangwei Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AdaTracker提出了一种自适应上下文策略学习框架，通过显式建模不同机器人形态的物理约束，实现零样本的跨形态主动视觉跟踪，在仿真和真实实验中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨机器人视觉跟踪方法需要为每种形态单独训练模型，可扩展性差且泛化能力有限，因此需要一种统一的、能适应不同机器人形态的主动视觉跟踪框架。

Method: 提出AdaTracker框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Embodiment Context Encoder，从历史轨迹推断形态特定的约束；(2) Context-Aware Policy，根据上下文表示动态调整控制策略。此外引入两个辅助目标函数以增强上下文识别的准确性和时间一致性。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，AdaTracker在跨形态泛化、样本效率和零样本自适应方面显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模形态约束的上下文学习，AdaTracker能够实现单模型跨多种机器人平台的零样本主动视觉跟踪，具有良好的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Realizing active visual tracking with a single unified model across diverse robots is challenging, as the physical constraints and motion dynamics vary drastically from one platform to another. Existing approaches typically train separate models for each embodiment, leading to poor scalability and limited generalization. To address this, we propose AdaTracker, an adaptive in-context policy learning framework that robustly tracks targets on diverse robot morphologies. Our key insight is to explicitly model embodiment-specific constraints through an Embodiment Context Encoder, which infers embodiment-specific constraints from history. This contextual representation dynamically modulates a Context-Aware Policy, enabling it to infer optimal control actions for unseen embodiments in a zero-shot manner. To enhance robustness, we introduce two auxiliary objectives to ensure accurate context identification and temporal consistency. Experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate that AdaTracker significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in cross-embodiment generalization, sample efficiency, and zero-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [164] [A Vision-Language-Action Model for Adaptive Ultrasound-Guided Needle Insertion and Needle Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20347)
*Yuelin Zhang,Qingpeng Ding,Longxiang Tang,Chengyu Fang,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种视觉-语言-动作(VLA)模型，用于机器人超声系统上的自适应自动化针头插入和追踪。该方法通过跨深度融合追踪头、追踪条件寄存器以及异步VLA管道，实现了实时端到端追踪和自适应插入控制，在追踪精度、插入成功率和手术时间上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声引导下的针头插入是重要但具有挑战性的操作，现有自动化方法依赖手工设计的模块化控制器，在复杂场景下性能下降严重。

Method: 提出VLA模型，包含三个关键组件：1) Cross-Depth Fusion (CDF)追踪头，融合浅层位置特征和深层语义特征实现实时端到端追踪；2) Tracking-Conditioning (TraCon)寄存器，用于参数高效的特征调节；3) 不确定性感知控制策略和异步VLA管道，实现自适应针头插入控制。

Result: 在针头追踪和插入实验中，该方法在追踪精度、插入成功率和手术时间上均持续优于最先进的追踪器和手动操作。

Conclusion: 该方法为机器人超声引导下的智能介入干预提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US)-guided needle insertion is a critical yet challenging procedure due to dynamic imaging conditions and difficulties in needle visualization. Many methods have been proposed for automated needle insertion, but they often rely on hand-crafted pipelines with modular controllers, whose performance degrades in challenging cases. In this paper, a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model is proposed for adaptive and automated US-guided needle insertion and tracking on a robotic ultrasound (RUS) system. This framework provides a unified approach to needle tracking and needle insertion control, enabling real-time, dynamically adaptive adjustment of insertion based on the obtained needle position and environment awareness. To achieve real-time and end-to-end tracking, a Cross-Depth Fusion (CDF) tracking head is proposed, integrating shallow positional and deep semantic features from the large-scale vision backbone. To adapt the pretrained vision backbone for tracking tasks, a Tracking-Conditioning (TraCon) register is introduced for parameter-efficient feature conditioning. After needle tracking, an uncertainty-aware control policy and an asynchronous VLA pipeline are presented for adaptive needle insertion control, ensuring timely decision-making for improved safety and outcomes. Extensive experiments on both needle tracking and insertion show that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art trackers and manual operation, achieving higher tracking accuracy, improved insertion success rates, and reduced procedure time, highlighting promising directions for RUS-based intelligent intervention.

</details>


### [165] [Bimanual Robot Manipulation via Multi-Agent In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20348)
*Alessio Palma,Indro Spinelli,Vignesh Prasad,Luca Scofano,Yufeng Jin,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiCICLe 是首个无需微调即可让标准 LLM 执行双机械臂少样本操作任务的框架，通过将双臂控制分解为多智能体领导者-追随者问题，引入 Arms' Debate 迭代优化和 LLM-as-Judge 评估机制，在 TWIN 基准测试中达到 71.1% 的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ICL 方法在处理双机械臂操作时，面临高维联合动作空间和紧密的臂间协调约束，会迅速超出标准上下文窗口的限制，亟需一种无需训练即可实现双臂协调控制的方法。

Method: 将双臂控制建模为多智能体领导者-追随者问题，解耦动作空间为顺序的、条件化的单臂预测；提出 Arms' Debate 迭代精炼过程；引入第三个 LLM-as-Judge 评估和选择最合理的协调轨迹。

Result: 在 TWIN 基准的 13 个任务上平均成功率达 71.1%，比最佳无训练基线高出 6.7 个百分点，并超越大多数有监督方法，且在全新任务上展现出强大的少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: BiCICLe 有效解决了 LLM 应用于双机械臂 ICL 操作中动作空间过大的问题，为无需微调的双臂协调控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful reasoning engines for embodied control. In particular, In-Context Learning (ICL) enables off-the-shelf, text-only LLMs to predict robot actions without any task-specific training while preserving their generalization capabilities. Applying ICL to bimanual manipulation remains challenging, as the high-dimensional joint action space and tight inter-arm coordination constraints rapidly overwhelm standard context windows. To address this, we introduce BiCICLe (Bimanual Coordinated In-Context Learning), the first framework that enables standard LLMs to perform few-shot bimanual manipulation without fine-tuning. BiCICLe frames bimanual control as a multi-agent leader-follower problem, decoupling the action space into sequential, conditioned single-arm predictions. This naturally extends to Arms' Debate, an iterative refinement process, and to the introduction of a third LLM-as-Judge to evaluate and select the most plausible coordinated trajectories. Evaluated on 13 tasks from the TWIN benchmark, BiCICLe achieves up to 71.1% average success rate, outperforming the best training-free baseline by 6.7 percentage points and surpassing most supervised methods. We further demonstrate strong few-shot generalization on novel tasks.

</details>


### [166] [Benefits of Low-Cost Bio-Inspiration in the Age of Overparametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20365)
*Kevin Godin-Dubois,Anil Yaman,Anna V. Kononova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在输入输出空间小且性能受限的机器人控制场景中，参数空间过大会阻碍而非促进学习过程。通过对比CPG和MLP两种范式在进化与强化学习训练协议下的表现，发现浅层MLP和密集连接CPG优于深层架构，并引入参数影响度指标证明强化学习所需的额外参数并未带来性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CPG和MLP被广泛应用于机器人控制，但很少有系统研究探讨大参数空间在小型输入输出空间中的相对优劣，特别是在性能有边界的情况下，更多参数可能反而阻碍学习。

Method: 对固定机器人形态（有限本体感知能力）分别在CPG和MLP两种生物启发范式下，使用进化算法和强化学习两种训练协议进行控制器优化。通过变化参数空间和多个奖励函数，观察不同架构的性能表现，并引入参数影响度（Parameter Impact）指标衡量参数增加对性能的实际贡献。

Result: 浅层MLP和密集连接CPG的性能优于深层MLP或Actor-Critic架构。强化学习所需的额外参数并未转化为更好的性能表现，进化策略因而更具优势。

Conclusion: 在小型输入输出空间且性能有边界的控制任务中，参数空间过大会阻碍学习；进化策略比强化学习更适合此类场景，因为后者增加的参数并未带来性能提升。

Abstract: While Central Pattern Generators (CPGs) and Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLP) are widely used paradigms in robot control, few systematic studies have been performed on the relative merits of large parameter spaces. In contexts where input and output spaces are small and performance is bounded, having more parameters to optimize may actively hinder the learning process instead of empowering it. To empirically measure this, we submit a given robot morphology, with limited proprioceptive capabilities, to controller optimization under two bio-inspired paradigms (CPGs and MLPs) with evolutionary- and reinforcement- trainer protocols. By varying parameter spaces across multiple reward functions, we observe that shallow MLPs and densely connected CPGs result in better performance when compared to deeper MLPs or Actor-Critic architectures. To account for the relationship between said performance and the number of parameters, we introduce a Parameter Impact metric which demonstrates that the additional parameters required by the reinforcement technique do not translate into better performance, thus favouring evolutionary strategies.

</details>


### [167] [OVPD: A Virtual-Physical Fusion Testing Dataset of OnSite Auton-omous Driving Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20423)
*Yuhang Zhang,Jiarui Zhang,Bowen Jian,Xin Zhou,Zhichao Lv,Peng Hang,Rongjie Yu,Ye Tian,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OVPD是一个虚实融合的自动驾驶测试数据集，包含20支队伍在15个原子场景链上近3小时的多模态数据，支持闭环测试和综合评估，可用于故障诊断与迭代改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶数据集缺乏真实车辆动力学反馈和闭环交互能力，难以反映实际部署水平，因此需要构建虚实融合、可交互的测试数据集。

Method: 基于实车在环测试，融合虚拟背景交通与车路感知，在试车场构建可控的闭环测试环境，采集车辆轨迹、控制指令及数字孪生环视观测等多模态数据。

Result: 发布了包含20个测试片段、近3小时多模态数据的OVPD数据集，涵盖15个原子场景，支持长尾决策验证及多维度评估。

Conclusion: OVPD为自动驾驶算法提供了可回放、可诊断、可闭环的虚实融合测试数据，有效弥补了现有数据集在部署就绪性评估上的不足。

Abstract: The rapid iteration of autonomous driving algorithms has created a growing demand for high-fidelity, replayable, and diagnosable testing data. However, many public datasets lack real vehicle dynamics feedback and closed-loop interaction with surrounding traffic and road infrastructure, limiting their ability to reflect deployment readiness. To address this gap, we present OVPD (OnSite Virtual-Physical Dataset), a virtual-physical fusion testing dataset released from the 2025 OnSite Autonomous Driving Challenge. Centered on real-vehicle-in-the-loop testing, OVPD integrates virtual background traffic with vehicle-infrastructure perception to build controllable and interactive closed-loop test environments on a proving ground. The dataset contains 20 testing clips from 20 teams over a scenario chain of 15 atomic scenarios, totaling nearly 3 hours of multi-modal data, including vehicle trajectories and states, control commands, and digital-twin-rendered surround-view observations. OVPD supports long-tail planning and decision-making validation, open-loop or platform-enabled closed-loop evaluation, and comprehensive assessment across safety, efficiency, comfort, rule compliance, and traffic impact, providing actionable evidence for failure diagnosis and iterative improvement. The dataset is available via: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Yuhang253820/Onsite_OPVD

</details>


### [168] [Lexicographic Minimum-Violation Motion Planning using Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20428)
*Patrick Halder,Lothar Kiltz,Hannes Homburger,Johannes Reuter,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将词典式STL最小违规运动规划转化为单目标标量优化问题的方法，通过非均匀量化和位移技术，结合扩展的MPPI求解器和新的谓词鲁棒性度量，实现了高效、可解释和可扩展的自动驾驶运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划常需满足多个条件冲突的规范，当无法同时满足所有规范时，需要最小违规运动规划。然而，规范的完全排序导致词典式优化问题，使用标准方法求解计算成本高。

Method: 1) 将多目标词典优化转化为单目标标量优化问题（非均匀量化+位移）；2) 扩展确定性模型预测路径积分（MPPI）求解器，无需二次输入成本；3) 引入结合空间和时间违规的新型谓词鲁棒性度量。

Result: 该方法在单目标求解器框架内提供了可解释且可扩展的词典式STL最小违规运动规划解决方案。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了词典式优化计算昂贵的难题，为自动驾驶运动规划提供了一种高效、可解释且可扩展的方案。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles often requires satisfying multiple conditionally conflicting specifications. In situations where not all specifications can be met simultaneously, minimum-violation motion planning maintains system operation by minimizing violations of specifications in accordance with their priorities. Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for rigorously defining these specifications and enables the quantitative evaluation of their violations. However, a total ordering of specifications yields a lexicographic optimization problem, which is typically computationally expensive to solve using standard methods. We address this problem by transforming the multi-objective lexicographic optimization problem into a single-objective scalar optimization problem using non-uniform quantization and bit-shifting. Specifically, we extend a deterministic model predictive path integral (MPPI) solver to efficiently solve optimization problems without quadratic input cost. Additionally, a novel predicate-robustness measure that combines spatial and temporal violations is introduced. Our results show that the proposed method offers an interpretable and scalable solution for lexicographic STL minimum-violation motion planning within a single-objective solver framework.

</details>


### [169] [VTouch++: A Multimodal Dataset with Vision-Based Tactile Enhancement for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20444)
*Qianxi Hua,Xinyue Li,Zheng Yan,Yang Li,Chi Zhang,Yongyao Li,Yufei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决灵巧双臂操作中缺乏物理交互信号数据集的问题，本文提出了VTOUCH数据集，利用基于视觉的触觉传感提供高保真物理交互信号，采用矩阵式任务设计支持系统化学习，并通过自动化数据采集管线确保可扩展性。实验验证了该数据集在跨模态检索和真机评估中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作尤其在接触密集的任务中仍面临挑战，主要原因是缺乏具备丰富物理交互信号、系统化任务组织和足够规模的数据集。

Method: 提出VTOUCH数据集，包括：(1) 基于视觉的触觉传感提供高保真物理交互信号；(2) 矩阵式任务设计支持系统化学习；(3) 自动化数据采集管线，覆盖真实世界、需求驱动的场景，确保可扩展性。

Result: 在跨模态检索和真机评估中进行了大量定量实验，并在多机器人、多策略、多任务上展示了可泛化的真实世界性能。

Conclusion: VTOUCH数据集有效解决了双臂操作中数据不足的问题，并在多个机器人平台和任务上验证了其泛化能力。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence has advanced rapidly in recent years; however, bimanual manipulation-especially in contact-rich tasks remains challenging. This is largely due to the lack of datasets with rich physical interaction signals, systematic task organization, and sufficient scale. To address these limitations, we introduce the VTOUCH dataset. It leverages vision based tactile sensing to provide high-fidelity physical interaction signals, adopts a matrix-style task design to enable systematic learning, and employs automated data collection pipelines covering real-world, demand-driven scenarios to ensure scalability. To further validate the effectiveness of the dataset, we conduct extensive quantitative experiments on cross-modal retrieval as well as real-robot evaluation. Finally, we demonstrate real-world performance through generalizable inference across multiple robots, policies, and tasks.

</details>


### [170] [MOMO: A framework for seamless physical, verbal, and graphical robot skill learning and adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20468)
*Markus Knauer,Edoardo Fiorini,Maximilian Mühlbauer,Stefan Schneyer,Promwat Angsuratanawech,Florian Samuel Lay,Timo Bachmann,Samuel Bustamante,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer,João Silvério,Thomas Eiband*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个三模态交互框架（触觉、语言、图形界面），结合LLM工具架构与KMP运动编码，实现工业机器人技能的灵活适配，并在7-DoF机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人需要非专家用户也能灵活适配不同任务和环境，但不同的适配方式需要不同的交互模态，现有方法单一模态难以兼顾。

Method: ①基于能量的意图检测；②基于工具架构的LLM（LLM选择预定义函数而非生成代码）实现安全自然语言适配；③KMP运动基元编码；④概率虚拟夹具引导示教；⑤遍历控制用于表面处理。

Result: 在Automatica 2025展会的7-DoF力控机器人上验证，表明工具式LLM架构能将技能适配从KMP推广到遍历控制，实现语音指令表面处理。

Conclusion: 所提多模态交互框架具备工业实用性，工具式LLM架构提升了自然语言适配的安全性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Industrial robot applications require increasingly flexible systems that non-expert users can easily adapt for varying tasks and environments. However, different adaptations benefit from different interaction modalities. We present an interactive framework that enables robot skill adaptation through three complementary modalities: kinesthetic touch for precise spatial corrections, natural language for high-level semantic modifications, and a graphical web interface for visualizing geometric relations and trajectories, inspecting and adjusting parameters, and editing via-points by drag-and-drop. The framework integrates five components: energy-based human-intention detection, a tool-based LLM architecture (where the LLM selects and parameterizes predefined functions rather than generating code) for safe natural language adaptation, Kernelized Movement Primitives (KMPs) for motion encoding, probabilistic Virtual Fixtures for guided demonstration recording, and ergodic control for surface finishing. We demonstrate that this tool-based LLM architecture generalizes skill adaptation from KMPs to ergodic control, enabling voice-commanded surface finishing. Validation on a 7-DoF torque-controlled robot at the Automatica 2025 trade fair demonstrates the practical applicability of our approach in industrial settings.

</details>


### [171] [Temporal Difference Calibration in Sequential Tasks: Application to Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20472)
*Shelly Francis-Meretzki,Mirco Mutti,Yaniv Romano,Aviv Tamar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的顺序校准方法，通过将时序差分（TD）值估计与Brier评分扩展相结合，实现了对部分轨迹任务的不确定性量化，并在仿真和真实机器人数据上取得了优于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在机器人顺序任务中缺乏有效的不确定性量化方法，尤其是在仅观察到部分轨迹时，校准问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出了顺序Brier评分作为校准度量，证明其风险最小化等价于VLA策略的价值函数，从而将校准与强化学习中的时序差分（TD）值估计联系起来，以此实现随时间演进的校准机制。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人数据上，TD校准方法优于现有最先进方法；同时发现经TD校准后，VLA模型单步动作概率也能提供具有竞争力的不确定性估计，这与先前研究结论不同。

Conclusion: 不确定性校准与强化学习价值估计之间存在理论联系，TD方法为VLA模型提供了有效的时序校准手段，且单步概率也能成为可靠的不确定性来源。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language-action (VLA) models for robotics have highlighted the importance of reliable uncertainty quantification in sequential tasks. However, assessing and improving calibration in such settings remains mostly unexplored, especially when only partial trajectories are observed. In this work, we formulate sequential calibration for episodic tasks, where task-success confidence is produced along an episode, while success is determined at the end of it. We introduce a sequential extension of the Brier score and show that, for binary outcomes, its risk minimizer coincides with the VLA policy's value function. This connection bridges uncertainty calibration and reinforcement learning, enabling the use of temporal-difference (TD) value estimation as a principled calibration mechanism over time. We empirically show that TD calibration improves performance relative to the state-of-the-art on simulated and real-robot data. Interestingly, we show that when calibrated using TD, the VLA's single-step action probabilities can yield competitive uncertainty estimates, in contrast to recent findings that employed different calibration techniques.

</details>


### [172] [Passive Variable Impedance For Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20557)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Nepomuk Werner,Ribin Balachandran,Thomas Hulin,João Silvério,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一框架，将变刚度阻抗控制和多目标控制器输出力/力矩的加权仲裁结合起来，识别闭环系统中的无源性违规并提供了被动化方法，从而稳定共享控制系统。该方法不限制刚度矩阵或仲裁因子的设计（可含非对角元素且时变），仿真和真实机器人实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享控制中常同时跟踪多个目标位姿，且刚度增益和权重随时间变化，现有方法可能导致闭环系统失去无源性（被动性），引发不稳定问题。作者旨在统一变刚度阻抗控制与多控制器输出仲裁，并解决稳定性保障问题。

Method: ① 将变刚度阻抗控制与多控制器输出力/力矩的缩放加法仲裁重构为统一框架。② 识别闭环系统中无源性违规的条件。③ 提出被动化方法（passivation techniques）来恢复系统无源性。④ 刚度矩阵和仲裁因子均可为矩阵形式（含非对角元素）且任意时变，不做约束限制。

Result: 仿真实验和多种真实机器人系统上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性，展示了不同场景下可依据共享控制需求选择的不同行为表现。

Conclusion: 该方法可用于稳定标准阻抗控制器，为开发新颖、灵活的共享控制方法提供了理论基础和实用工具，且对刚度设计和仲裁设计没有限制，适用性广。

Abstract: Shared Control methods often use impedance control to track target poses in a robotic manipulator. The guidance behavior of such controllers is shaped by the used stiffness gains, which can be varying over time to achieve an adaptive guiding. When multiple target poses are tracked at the same time with varying importance, the corresponding output wrenches have to be arbitrated with weightings changing over time. In this work, we study the stabilization of both variable stiffness in impedance control as well as the arbitration of different controllers through a scaled addition of their output wrenches, reformulating both into a holistic framework. We identify passivity violations in the closed loop system and provide methods to passivate the system. The resulting approach can be used to stabilize standard impedance controllers, allowing for the development of novel and flexible shared control methods. We do not constrain the design of stiffness matrices or arbitration factors; both can be matrix-valued including off-diagonal elements and change arbitrarily over time. The proposed methods are furthermore validated in simulation as well as in real robot experiments on different systems, proving their effectiveness and showcasing different behaviors which can be utilized depending on the requirements of the shared control approach.

</details>


### [173] [Kinematic Optimization of Phalanx Length Ratios in Robotic Hands Using Potential Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20686)
*HyoJae Kang,Joonho Lee,Jeongdo Ahn,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于运动学结构内在灵巧性的五指机器人手部指节长度比优化框架，采用全局可操作性、工作空间体积、重叠工作空间体积和指尖灵敏度等指标，通过加权目标函数寻找最优设计，并揭示了各指节对灵巧性的非均等贡献及权重系数的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人手部设计阶段，缺乏在不定义具体物体或操作任务的情况下定量评估指节长度比对灵巧性影响的方法。

Method: 采用全局可操作性、工作空间体积、重叠工作空间体积和指尖灵敏度作为评估指标，通过加权目标函数在约束条件下优化。使用体素表示离散化可达工作空间，均匀间隔离散化关节运动，在拇指和其他手指的设计集上进行优化，排除不产生重叠工作空间的设计组合。

Result: 各指节对整体灵巧性的贡献不均等，并识别出影响各指节的因素。权重系数的选择不一定导致单个性能指标的直接最大化，原因是设计空间内评估度量的非均匀分布。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种系统分析可达性、灵巧性和可控性之间权衡的方法，可作为多指机器人手部运动学设计的实用指南。

Abstract: In the design stage of robotic hands, it is not straightforward to quantitatively evaluate the effect of phalanx length ratios on dexterity without defining specific objects or manipulation tasks. Therefore, this study presents a framework for optimizing the phalanx length ratios of a five-finger robotic hand based on potential dexterity within a kinematic structure. The proposed method employs global manipulability, workspace volume, overlap workspace volume, and fingertip sensitivity as evaluation metrics, and identifies optimal design configurations using a weighted objective function under given constraints. The reachable workspace is discretized using a voxel-based representation, and joint motions are discretized at uniform intervals for evaluation. The optimization is performed over design sets for both the thumb and the other fingers, and design combinations that do not generate overlap workspace are excluded. The results show that each phalanx does not contribute equally to the overall dexterity, and the factors influencing each phalanx are identified. In addition, it is observed that the selection of weighting coefficients does not necessarily lead to the direct maximization of individual performance metrics, due to the non-uniform distribution of evaluation measures within the design space. The proposed framework provides a systematic approach to analyze the trade-offs among reachability, dexterity, and controllability, and can serve as a practical guideline for the kinematic design of multi-fingered robotic hands.

</details>


### [174] [FingerEye: Continuous and Unified Vision-Tactile Sensing for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20689)
*Zhixuan Xu,Yichen Li,Xuanye Wu,Tianyu Qiu,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FingerEye，一种集成双目RGB相机的紧凑型触觉传感器，能在操作全过程（接触前、接触中、接触后）提供连续的视觉-触觉反馈，并基于此开发了视觉-触觉模仿学习策略，实现了多种灵巧操作任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感器（如GelSight等）仅在接触后提供反馈，无法在接触前和接触瞬间提供感知信息，限制了机器人在交互过程中精确调整动作的能力。因此需要一种能在整个交互过程提供连续反馈的感知方案。

Method: 1) 硬件设计：集成双目RGB相机，提供近距离视觉感知与隐式立体深度；接触时通过标记点位姿估计感知柔性环结构的形变，作为接触力/力矩的代理。2) 算法设计：基于视觉-触觉模仿学习策略，融合多个FingerEye传感器信号；构建数字孪生平台，通过真实演示与增强模拟观测结合的表征学习提升策略泛化性。

Result: 在多种灵巧操作任务上验证了有效性，包括硬币立起、芯片拾取、信件拿取、注射器操作等，展现出对不同物体属性和交互模式的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FingerEye通过提供从接触前到接触后无缝过渡的连续视觉-触觉反馈，结合模仿学习策略与数字孪生增强，显著提升了灵巧操作的精度与泛化能力。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic manipulation requires comprehensive perception across all phases of interaction: pre-contact, contact initiation, and post-contact. Such continuous feedback allows a robot to adapt its actions throughout interaction. However, many existing tactile sensors, such as GelSight and its variants, only provide feedback after contact is established, limiting a robot's ability to precisely initiate contact. We introduce FingerEye, a compact and cost-effective sensor that provides continuous vision-tactile feedback throughout the interaction process. FingerEye integrates binocular RGB cameras to provide close-range visual perception with implicit stereo depth. Upon contact, external forces and torques deform a compliant ring structure; these deformations are captured via marker-based pose estimation and serve as a proxy for contact wrench sensing. This design enables a perception stream that smoothly transitions from pre-contact visual cues to post-contact tactile feedback. Building on this sensing capability, we develop a vision-tactile imitation learning policy that fuses signals from multiple FingerEye sensors to learn dexterous manipulation behaviors from limited real-world data. We further develop a digital twin of our sensor and robot platform to improve policy generalization. By combining real demonstrations with visually augmented simulated observations for representation learning, the learned policies become more robust to object appearance variations. Together, these design aspects enable dexterous manipulation across diverse object properties and interaction regimes, including coin standing, chip picking, letter retrieving, and syringe manipulation. The hardware design, code, appendix, and videos are available on our project website: https://nus-lins-lab.github.io/FingerEyeWeb/

</details>


### [175] [A Kinematic Framework for Evaluating Pinch Configurations in Robotic Hand Design without Object or Contact Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20692)
*HyoJae Kang,Joonho Lee,Hyunmok Jung,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于指尖工作空间交互的机器人手部抓取能力运动学评估方法，无需物体几何或接触力模型即可评估抓取能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取评估方法依赖物体几何或接触力模型，在机器人手部设计早期阶段适用性有限，因此需要一种仅基于运动学结构的评估方法。

Method: 首先从手指关节构型计算每个指尖的可达工作空间，然后通过评估指尖对之间的关系来检测可行的抓取构型。

Result: 在四种不同的手部运动学结构上进行了分析，验证了该方法能够仅基于运动学结构评估抓取能力。

Conclusion: 该评估框架可作为设计阶段比较不同机器人手部设计和分析抓取能力的有用工具。

Abstract: Evaluating the pinch capability of a robotic hand is important for understanding its functional dexterity. However, many existing grasp evaluation methods rely on object geometry or contact force models, which limits their applicability during the early stages of robotic hand design. This study proposes a kinematic evaluation method for analyzing pinch configurations of robotic hands based on interactions between fingertip workspaces. First, the reachable workspace of each fingertip is computed from the joint configurations of the fingers. Then, feasible pinch configurations are detected by evaluating the relationships between fingertip pairs. Since the proposed method does not require information about object geometry or contact force models, the pinch capability of a robotic hand can be evaluated solely based on its kinematic structure. In addition, analyses are performed on four different kinematic structures of the hand to investigate their impact on the pinch configurations. The proposed evaluation framework can serve as a useful tool for comparing different robotic hand designs and analyzing pinch capability during the design stage.

</details>


### [176] [Visual-Tactile Peg-in-Hole Assembly Learning from Peg-out-of-Hole Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20712)
*Yongqiang Zhao,Xuyang Zhang,Zhuo Chen,Matteo Leonetti,Emmanouil Spyrakos-Papastavridis,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用逆向任务（拔销出孔）来辅助学习插销入孔装配技能的视觉-触觉联合学习框架。通过时间反转PooH轨迹并引入动作随机化来生成PiH的专家数据，实验表明该方法在成功率和接触力控制上均优于直接强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PiH装配任务虽然重要但强化学习需要大量探索，学习效率低。作者观察到其逆向任务PooH（拔销出孔）天然更容易执行，因为只需克服摩擦力而无需精确对准，因此希望利用PooH的数据来加速PiH的学习。

Method: 1) 将PiH和PooH统一建模为POMDP，共享视觉-触觉观测空间；2) 先训练视觉-触觉PooH策略；3) 将PooH轨迹进行时间反转并加入动作随机化，生成PiH的专家数据；4) 利用这些数据辅助PiH策略学习，其中视觉用于引导接近，触觉用于补偿对准误差。

Result: 视觉-触觉策略的接触力比单模态方法低6.4%；在已知物体上成功率达87.5%，在未见物体上达77.1%，比从头训练的RL方法高出18.1%的成功率。

Conclusion: 利用逆向任务PooH来辅助PiH学习是有效的，视觉-触觉多模态融合优于单模态方法，且该框架具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Peg-in-hole (PiH) assembly is a fundamental yet challenging robotic manipulation task. While reinforcement learning (RL) has shown promise in tackling such tasks, it requires extensive exploration. In this paper, we propose a novel visual-tactile skill learning framework for the PiH task that leverages its inverse task, i.e., peg-out-of-hole (PooH) disassembly, to facilitate PiH learning. Compared to PiH, PooH is inherently easier as it only needs to overcome existing friction without precise alignment, making data collection more efficient. To this end, we formulate both PooH and PiH as Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) in a unified environment with shared visual-tactile observation space. A visual-tactile PooH policy is first trained; its trajectories, containing kinematic, visual and tactile information, are temporally reversed and action-randomized to provide expert data for PiH. In the policy learning, visual sensing facilitates the peg-hole approach, while tactile measurements compensate for peg-hole misalignment. Experiments across diverse peg-hole geometries show that the visual-tactile policy attains 6.4% lower contact forces than its single-modality counterparts, and that our framework achieves average success rates of 87.5% on seen objects and 77.1% on unseen objects, outperforming direct RL methods that train PiH policies from scratch by 18.1% in success rate. Demos, code, and datasets are available at https://sites.google.com/view/pooh2pih.

</details>


### [177] [ALAS: Adaptive Long-Horizon Action Synthesis via Async-pathway Stream Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20721)
*Yutong Shen,Hangxu Liu,Lei Zhang,Penghui Liu,Yinqi Liu,Liuxiang Yang,Tongtong Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ALAS框架，通过受大脑"where-what"双通路机制启发的双流解耦学习，解决了长时域人-场景交互任务中环境与技能耦合导致泛化能力差的问题，在多个任务上实现了23%的子任务成功率和29%的执行效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预训练子任务的技能链式拼接，环境观察与自身状态紧密耦合，无法泛化到新的环境与技能组合，难以完成跨域的长时域人-场景交互任务。

Method: 提出ALAS跨域学习框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 环境学习模块（空间理解），负责物体功能、空间关系和场景语义，实现环境-自身完全解耦的跨域迁移；2) 技能学习模块（任务执行），处理关节自由度、运动模式等自身状态信息，通过独立运动模式编码实现跨技能迁移。灵感来源于大脑"where-what"双通路机制。

Result: 在多种长时域人-场景交互任务上，相比现有方法，子任务平均成功率提升23%，平均执行效率提升29%。

Conclusion: ALAS通过生物启发的双流解耦方法有效解决了跨域长时域任务中的泛化问题，环境与技能的独立编码是实现这一目标的关键设计。

Abstract: Long-Horizon (LH) tasks in Human-Scene Interaction (HSI) are complex multi-step tasks that require continuous planning, sequential decision-making, and extended execution across domains to achieve the final goal. However, existing methods heavily rely on skill chaining by concatenating pre-trained subtasks, with environment observations and self-state tightly coupled, lacking the ability to generalize to new combinations of environments and skills, failing to complete various LH tasks across domains. To solve this problem, this paper presents ALAS, a cross-domain learning framework for LH tasks via biologically inspired dual-stream disentanglement. Inspired by the brain's "where-what" dual pathway mechanism, ALAS comprises two core modules: i) an environment learning module for spatial understanding, which captures object functions, spatial relationships, and scene semantics, achieving cross-domain transfer through complete environment-self disentanglement; ii) a skill learning module for task execution, which processes self-state information including joint degrees of freedom and motor patterns, enabling cross-skill transfer through independent motor pattern encoding. We conducted extensive experiments on various LH tasks in HSI scenes. Compared with existing methods, ALAS can achieve an average subtasks success rate improvement of 23\% and average execution efficiency improvement of 29\%.

</details>


### [178] [A Hough transform approach to safety-aware scalar field mapping using Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20799)
*Muzaffar Qureshi,Trivikram Satharasi,Tochukwu E. Ogri,Kyle Volle,Rushikesh Kamalapurkar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合高斯过程（GP）与霍夫变换（HT）的框架，使自主机器人在避让危险高强度区域的同时，安全高效地完成未知标量场测绘任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不安全环境中（高强度区域被定义为超过安全阈值的区域），传统测绘方法可能使机器人进入危险区域。因此需要一种既能测绘标量场、又能主动避开高风险区域的策略。

Method: 1）使用高斯过程对标量场建模，实现贝叶斯推理并获得预测均值与不确定性的闭式表达；2）利用霍夫变换实时估计高强度区域的空间结构（基于GP后验）；3）设计基于概率安全保证的采样策略，引导机器人前往安全测量点；4）利用估计的高强度区域生成安全运动规划。

Result: 通过两个数值仿真实验以及一个使用轮式移动机器人测绘光强场的室内实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够在避免危险高强度区域的前提下，实现安全、高效的标量场测绘，为机器人在不安全环境中的自主作业提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a framework for mapping unknown scalar fields using a sensor-equipped autonomous robot operating in unsafe environments. The unsafe regions are defined as regions of high-intensity, where the field value exceeds a predefined safety threshold. For safe and efficient mapping of the scalar field, the sensor-equipped robot must avoid high-intensity regions during the measurement process. In this paper, the scalar field is modeled as a sample from a Gaussian process (GP), which enables Bayesian inference and provides closed-form expressions for both the predictive mean and the uncertainty. Concurrently, the spatial structure of the high-intensity regions is estimated in real-time using the Hough transform (HT), leveraging the evolving GP posterior. A safe sampling strategy is then employed to guide the robot towards safe measurement locations, using probabilistic safety guarantees on the evolving GP posterior. The estimated high-intensity regions also facilitate the design of safe motion plans for the robot. The effectiveness of the approach is verified through two numerical simulation studies and an indoor experiment for mapping a light-intensity field using a wheeled mobile robot.

</details>


### [179] [PokeVLA: Empowering Pocket-Sized Vision-Language-Action Model with Comprehensive World Knowledge Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20834)
*Yupeng Zheng,Xiang Li,Songen Gu,Yuhang Zheng,Shuai Tian,Weize Li,Linbo Wang,Senyu Fei,Pengfei Li,Yinfeng Gao,Zebin Xing,Yilun Chen,Qichao Zhang,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PokeVLA是一个轻量级基础模型，通过两阶段训练（先预训练视觉语言模型，再注入操作相关知识）提升了机器人操纵的效率、空间感知和鲁棒性，在LIBERO-Plus基准和真实部署中达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型效率低，缺乏高层知识和空间感知能力，难以高效地融合视觉-语言理解与动作学习。

Method: 提出两阶段训练范式：1) 在包含空间定位、可供性、具身推理的240万样本多模态数据集上预训练轻量视觉语言模型PokeVLM；2) 通过多视角目标感知语义学习、几何对齐和动作专家模块将操作相关表示注入动作空间。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus基准和真实机器人部署中达到SOTA性能，在成功率和鲁棒性上超越基线方法。

Conclusion: PokeVLA证明了轻量级模型结合精心设计的预训练和动作注入策略，能够有效提升机器人操纵的效率和泛化能力，且将开源促进社区发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have opened new avenues for robot manipulation, yet existing methods exhibit limited efficiency and a lack of high-level knowledge and spatial awareness. To address these challenges, we propose PokeVLA, a lightweight yet powerful foundation model for embodied manipulation that effectively infuses vision-language understanding into action learning. Our framework introduces a two-stage training paradigm: first, we pre-train a compact vision-language model (PokeVLM) on a curated multimodal dataset of 2.4M samples encompassing spatial grounding, affordance, and embodied reasoning tasks; second, we inject manipulation-relevant representations into the action space through multi-view goal-aware semantics learning, geometry alignment, and a novel action expert. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on the LIBERO-Plus benchmark and in real-world deployment, outperforming comparable baselines in success rate and robustness under diverse perturbations. To foster reproducibility and community progress, we will open-source our code, model weights, and the scripts for the curated pre-training dataset. Project page: https://getterupper.github.io/PokeVLA

</details>
